# 2026-06-28 Static React Owner Coverage Matrix

Status: generated audit evidence
Date: 2026-06-28
Source: `yona-original/app/views/**`, `frontend/src/routes/**`

This matrix mechanically maps each legacy Scala template to likely React route owner files and checks whether legacy DOM/message anchors appear in those React sources. It is not rendered UI parity evidence and it does not close any page. It is a gap triage aid for the exhaustive rebuild audit.

Legacy template count: 242
React route source count: 125

## Status Summary

| status | count | meaning |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| static-anchor-candidate | 134 | High static anchor overlap; still requires rendered verification and legacy UX review. |
| partial-static-match | 93 | Some legacy anchors are present, but remaining anchors need template-level review. |
| needs-static-review | 3 | Low static overlap against current React owners; likely rebuild/gap queue item. |
| no-static-anchors | 12 | Legacy template exposes no extracted static DOM/message anchors; inspect behavior manually. |
| needs-owner-map | 0 | No plausible React owner was found by the current map. |
